Result: 1. 仅31.1%的专家标注提及点位于注入管道之外；2. 在重叠段，注入读数与人工翻译一致率达97.8%，远高于独立段的70.1%；3. KB注入带来的增益仅限于共享注入资源的重叠段，在独立段增益为零或负值；4. 注入后的保留率集中在狭窄区间（0.910-0.996），表明报告的增益是 prior performance 的补数，较弱模型看似改善更显著，实为测量偏差。

Conclusion: 现有的基于知识库注入的评估指标存在严重的自指循环问题，夸大了模型性能。真正的翻译质量应通过独立于注入管道的专家标注来评估，该研究提出的方法能有效区分模型真实能力与评估偏差。

Abstract: The Seungjeongwon Ilgi, a UNESCO Memory of the World record, is only 37.4% translated, and the most conspicuous failure mode in automatic translation is the person name -- a misread name corrupts the historical fact rather than merely the surface. Low-resource historical domains have no expert gold standard for entity translation, so practitioners substitute a knowledge base (KB) for the gold. That KB is the same resource injected into the system: scoring becomes self-referential and the metric measures instruction compliance rather than translation quality.
  We measure this loop. Using expert person-name annotations from the National Institute of Korean History as a gold independent of the injection pipeline, we hold the entity set fixed and vary only the provenance of the correct reading. Of 527 expert-annotated mentions, only 31.1% lie outside the injection pipeline, and the residual loop is not uniform -- in the overlapping segment the injected reading agrees with the human translation 97.8% of the time against 70.1% in the independent one, so the segment that looks healthiest is the one the loop is holding up.
  Across four models, a difference-in-differences analysis shows the gain from KB injection is confined to the segment whose gold shares the injected resource; in the independent segment it is at or below zero. Post-injection preservation clusters in a narrow 0.910-0.996 band even though baseline capability differs fivefold, so the reported gain is the complement of prior performance and weaker models appear to improve more dramatically. On an independent sample built by removing the construction filter, the measure replicates within model (overlapping intervals) while discriminating between models (non-overlapping intervals) -- it reflects a property of the model, not of the sample.

</details>


### [141] [LazyTrain: Limited-resource Allocation toward Zero-waste Yield Optimization in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11919)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LazyTrain，通过混合整数调度优化层流执行器及8-bit算子，在有限硬件上高效训练大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卸载系统存在固定检查点和放置启发式规则，导致关键路径通信暴露，限制了在受限硬件上的训练效率。

Method: 将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算及CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠建模为混合整数调度问题；结合8-bit优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪的Hybrid 8-bit算子以平衡内存与计算开销。

Result: H800上持续TFLOPS提升1.24倍，RTX 3090上最大可行批量大小增加；Qwen3.6-27B实验中达到219.95 TFLOPS，峰值显存占用仅68.84 GB。

Conclusion: LazyTrain有效解决了大模型训练的调度瓶颈，显著提升了低资源环境下的训练吞吐量和可行性。

Abstract: Training large language models on limited hardware is increasingly a scheduling problem across GPU compute, host memory, PCIe transfer, and storage bandwidth. Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTrain formulates checkpoint selection, activation placement, recomputation, and CPU-GPU-NVMe communication overlap as a mixed-integer scheduling problem, then executes the solved policy during training. It further couples 8-bit optimizer states with fast gradient clipping as a single Hybrid 8-bit operator: state compression reduces optimizer-state memory, while fast clipping counteracts the additional CPU-side update overhead. Across H800 experiments from Qwen2.5-3B to Qwen3.6-27B, LazyTrain improves sustained TFLOPS over matched baselines runs by approximately 1.24$\times$; RTX 3090 experiments likewise increase the maximum feasible batch size by one at each model scale. In the primary Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA run, LazyTrain reaches 219.95 TFLOPS and 1361 tokens/s at batch size 72, peaks at 68.84\,GB of GPU memory, and obtains 95.42\% exact-match accuracy on the full evaluation split. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/LazyTrain.

</details>


### [142] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Spark-to-Paper系统，作为现有代码助手中的13个可组合技能，实现端到端的研究论文生成。该系统通过分离模型判断与确定性操作、实验规划与报告，结合完整性检查和自我批评，解决了长流程生成的一致性和可靠性问题，实现了高引用有效性和图表可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将研究想法转化为完整论文不仅需要文本生成，还需文献检索、实验设计与执行、基于证据修正主张、生成出版级图表及保持长流程一致性。现有系统缺乏将这些步骤整合为轻量级、无需独立代理平台的工作流的能力。

Method: 构建Spark-to-Paper系统，包含13个可组合技能，集成于现有代码助手。核心策略包括：1. 分离模型判断与可直接执行的确定性操作；2. 分离实验规划与报告，先指定所需证据再观察结果，并根据测量结果修订主张；3. 结合确定性完整性检查与自我批评以抑制“自我否定循环”（Self-Refutation Loop）；4. 使用程序化绘图生成可编辑矢量图，代码重建生成方法图。

Result: 在8个受控研究主题中测试：引用有效性达99.5%，图表可编辑性达96.4%。完整完整性与审查堆栈将伪造检测率从单遍草稿的14%提升至92%，对抗性审查精度达74%。平均成本为每篇手稿8.1美元，耗时3.2小时，消耗11.9M tokens。

Conclusion: 端到端的研究论文生成可以作为轻量级、可组合的工作流嵌入现有代码助手中实现，同时通过将实验证据置于核心地位，确保主张被接受、修订或放弃的科学严谨性。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [143] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了通过阻止模型查看选项标签来消除多项选择题（MCQ）中位置偏差的策略，发现单纯去除位置影响并不能可靠提升准确率，且存在性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多项选择题基准测试分数混淆了知识掌握与对选项顺序的敏感性，导致其作为模型知识衡量标准不可靠。

Method: 测试两种减轻偏差策略：1）先生成答案再匹配的方法；2）独立评分各选项以构建无位置偏见的方法。通过完整分解分析识别瓶颈，并比较不同配置下的表现。

Result: 两种减轻偏差策略均未能可靠提升准确率。瓶颈在于“隐藏选项”而非匹配步骤。唯一能稳定匹配基线性能的配置是向模型展示所有选项并使用LLM进行匹配。完全消除位置影响未带来可靠的准确率增益，而循环置换有时能提升性能。两阶段提示中的召回不平衡聚合指标和直接顺序敏感性指标均未显示出可靠的去偏效果。

Conclusion: 仅仅消除多项选择题中的位置影响力并不足以保证模型性能的显著提升，现有去偏策略在提高准确率方面效果有限，需进一步探索更有效的评估或优化方法。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>


### [144] [Benchmarking Trustworthiness of SLMs: Pre-trained vs. Compressed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11981)
*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究全面评估了小型语言模型（SLMs）在公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理方面的可信度。研究发现，量化比剪枝更能保持可信度，且通过量化压缩可靠的大模型或从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏，能产生比从头训练的小模型更具可信度和适应性的SLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型融入现实应用，确保其可信度至关重要，但如何构建可信的SLMs仍是一个未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 对多种压缩方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏）构建的SLMs在多个维度（公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、伦理）上进行综合评估，对比其与从头训练的小模型的可信度差异。

Result: 1. 量化在保持可信度方面显著优于剪枝；2. 通过量化压缩可靠大模型得到的SLMs，其可信度和适应性优于从头训练的小模型；3. 从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏可进一步提升SLMs的可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发和部署可信的小型语言模型提供了实践指导和研究基础。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [145] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A-CRC-QA框架，通过后验校准解决大语言模型不确定性量化问题，在保持答案保留率的同时提升回答可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有启发式不确定性评分无法完美区分正确与错误预测，且固定阈值缺乏对接受答案错误率的统计控制。

Method: 将选择条件误差控制重构为线性期望约束，应用受保形风险控制启发的单调化经验风险校准程序，构建模型无关的后验校准框架。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA数据集上验证了该方法在开放式和封闭式问答中的适用性，相比未校准和基于置信度界限的基线，实现了可靠性与保留率的更优权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA无需额外训练，可结合不同不确定性估计器，针对渐近风险控制提供了有效的不确定性感知选择性问答解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [146] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的孟加拉语多方言神经机器翻译系统，构建了包含12种方言的最大规模平行语料库，并通过DoRA微调BanglaT5模型实现了SOTA性能，同时开源了数据和部署了Web应用以促进数字包容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决孟加拉语中因2.4亿用户使用的多种地区方言与标准口语孟加拉语在音系、形态和词汇上存在显著差异，导致现有NLP模型（如NMT和LLM）在处理低资源方言时性能严重下降的问题。

Method: 1. 构建包含51,531个非空平行句子对的多方言平行语料库，涵盖12种方言及5种新加入方言的专家验证数据；2. 开发无需经过标准枢纽的多方向翻译系统；3. 使用Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA)技术对BanglaT5序列到序列架构进行微调；4. 进行跨方言迁移分析和数据集扩展研究；5. 部署INT8量化模型为开源Web应用。

Result: 微调后的BanglaT5模型取得了29.26 BLEU和57.26 chrF++的SOTA翻译性能，优于NLLB-200和mBART-50，并保持了形态连贯性；确立了低资源方言适应的经验阈值；建立了公开可用的数据集。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建大规模多方言语料库和优化模型训练方法，有效解决了孟加拉语地区方言翻译难题，提升了低资源语言处理性能，并通过开源资源和工具促进了边缘化方言社区的数字包容。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [147] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为偏好树优化（PTO）的新框架，结合带有前瞻的偏好树生成和直接偏好优化（DPO），旨在解决小数据量下目标导向对话系统的训练难题。在动机访谈（MI）领域通过虚拟患者模拟实验证明，该方法显著提升了会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标，且更深的前瞻配置能带来更稳定、高效的对话策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对专业领域中数据稀缺的问题，开发能够进行多轮、目标导向对话的系统仍面临巨大挑战；现有方法难以在有限数据下有效迭代提升智能体模型的性能。

Method: 提出了偏好树优化（PTO）框架，利用“带前瞻的偏好树”方法生成偏好数据；结合虚拟患者和预言机评估器模拟对话以构建丰富的偏好数据集；将生成的偏好数据与直接偏好优化（DPO）相结合，通过迭代训练周期增强智能体的决策能力。

Result: 在动机访谈（MI）领域的实验中，PTO框架训练的模型在会话满意度（session satisfaction）和工作联盟（working alliance）等关键指标上均一致优于基线模型；引入前瞻模拟改善了长期规划能力和对话策略，且更深的前瞻配置产生了最稳定和高分的结果。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了数据稀缺问题，推动了目标导向领域对话系统的发展，证明了结合前瞻模拟与DPO在提升智能体决策能力和对话质量方面的有效性。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [148] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过回顾NLP中的视角概念，定义区分属性并提出线性层级结构，以澄清立场、情感等概念间的关系，帮助研究者根据目标选择合适的操作化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多种NLP领域涉及视角（如立场、情感、框架、论点），但这些操作性概念之间的确切关系尚不明确，缺乏深层理论理解阻碍了更有效的研究。

Method: 综述NLP中视角的概念空间，定义一组有助于区分视角相关概念的属性，并据此构建一个沿单一轴线性组织的概念层级结构。

Result: 提出了一个原则性的概念层级结构，明确了不同视角相关概念之间的关系和组织方式。

Conclusion: 该层级结构有助于研究人员在领域中导航，并根据具体研究目标选择对齐的视角操作化方法，从而提升研究的精确性和有效性。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [149] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QV-PIC框架，通过离线编译视觉缓存和在线双分辨率重用机制，解决RAG中基于图像缓存的质量下降问题，在提升准确率的同时显著降低首字延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）中存在重复计算冗余；位置无关缓存（PIC）虽能复用KV缓存，但将文本渲染为图像会因上下文不匹配和细粒度信息丢失导致严重质量退化，且现有修复方法需在线重算或无法恢复细节。

Method: 提出查询感知的双分辨率PIC重用框架QV-PIC：1. 离线阶段：利用模型原生对话模板前缀编译视觉缓存，避免在线重算并提升质量；2. 在线阶段：低分辨率保留全局上下文，高分辨率预算内根据查询相关性分数恢复细粒度文本证据。

Result: 在六个任务上，相比原始渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6分，缩小至与原始文本PIC相当的水平，并超越优化后的文本PIC 2.58分；TTFT（首字延迟）降低17.2%；相比完整预填充，TTFT减少83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC有效解决了视觉缓存中的表示特异性差距，在保持视觉压缩效率优势的同时，实现了接近甚至优于文本PIC的性能表现，大幅提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [150] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAG（SQL检索增强生成），一种无需构建全局知识图谱的结构化检索架构，通过事件-实体索引和隐式超边保留n元关系，显著提升多跳推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决主流密集检索在处理结构化约束和多跳推理时的局限性，以及现有基于图的方法存在的语义碎片化、维护成本高和增量更新复杂等问题。

Method: 将文档组织为事件-实体索引，每个块表示为包含实体的完整事件（形成保留n元关系的隐式超边）；查询时利用共享实体作为连接键动态获取查询范围内的邻居事件集合，同时保持证据块的原始完整性。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue基准测试中取得最佳检索和端到端QA性能，且随着推理链复杂度增加优势扩大；在MuSiQue上Recall@5达到80.36%，比最强基线高出11.52分。

Conclusion: 该工作为支持LLM智能体在持续增长的组织知识中进行检索和推理的知识基础设施铺平了道路。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [151] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统研究层交错混合线性注意力大语言模型（HLA LLMs）中的大规模激活（MAs），发现其具有“预注意力尖峰”和“尖峰间平台”两种形态，并揭示了其随架构变化及门控机制影响的演化规律与底层机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在填补对层交错混合线性注意力大语言模型中大规模激活现象的系统性研究空白，理解其在不同架构配置下的表现及其与全注意力模型的关联。

Method: 1. 跨五类线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域及参数量从1.2B到397B的开源混合模型进行系统性分析；2. 在高达1.3B参数的基于GDN的混合模型上进行控制预训练实验；3. 通过系统性异常值分析探究大规模激活的生命周期机制。

Result: 1. 发现MA在完整注意力层前形成预注意力尖峰（PAS），并在中间线性注意力层持续形成尖峰间平台（ISP）；随着全注意力密度增加，PAS通过ISP连接，恢复为稳定形态；2. 该组织模式在不同架构和数据域中具有高度可重复性；3. 全注意力输出门控能显著抑制MA绝对幅值但不改变其层级结构，移除GDN门控仅导致小幅放大；4. PAS遵循局部写-汇-取消过程，而ISP的持久性与延迟取消一致。

Conclusion: 层交错HLA LLMs中的大规模激活具有特定的、可预测的形态学特征，其演化受注意力密度和门控机制调控，且可通过统一的“取消时机”生命周期理论解释，最终在全注意力极限下回归经典形态。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [152] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“信息丰富悖论”，指出在预训练和微调中，过长的上下文窗口虽能提升特定任务表现，但会减少模型对知识的参数化编码，转而过度依赖上下文注意力机制。这导致模型在测试时缺乏上下文或遇到误导信息时鲁棒性下降，表明无限扩展上下文并非简单增加数据量即可解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“训练更长上下文仅通过提供 richer evidence 来提升模型性能”的隐含假设，探究上下文窗口如何塑造模型的两种学习模式：参数化内化（parametric internalization）与情境化（contextualization）。

Method: 1. 提出“信息丰富悖论”假说；2. 在预训练阶段使用长文档进行实验，观察不同上下文窗口大小对语言建模、自然语言理解和闭卷多项选择问答的影响；3. 在监督微调阶段分析任务相关训练上下文对支持上下文存在或缺失/误导情况下的性能影响；4. 通过机制分析和因果干预，追踪梯度压力在前馈网络与注意力模块间的转移。

Result: 1. 预训练中，增加上下文窗口仅在中间最优值之前提升性能，之后性能持续下降；2. 微调中，更多任务相关上下文提升了有支持上下文时的性能，但在无上下文或上下文误导时降低了鲁棒性；3. 机制上，富含信息的上下文将梯度压力从负责参数化知识的前馈网络转移到注意力模块，增加了推理时对上下文的依赖。

Conclusion: 验证了信息丰富悖论，即丰富的相关信息会降低模型参数化编码该信息的动力，从而增加对上下文的依赖。这表明向近乎无限的上下文扩展并非仅仅提供更多数据的问题，即使高质量长上下文数据充足，也需谨慎处理上下文长度的影响。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [153] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多智能体强化学习中单一LLM模拟器导致的“模式崩溃”和泛化失败问题，提出了推理时的Verbalized Sampling和训练时的Co-Training两种解决方案，显著提升了策略在未见模拟器和真实用户上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单个大语言模型（LLM）模拟用户行为的多智能体强化学习方法存在系统性泛化失败问题，原因是模拟器发生模式崩溃，导致训练出的策略过度拟合于利用模拟器主导模式的狭窄策略，难以迁移到新的模拟器和真实用户。

Method: 1. 理论形式化了这种崩溃现象；2. 提出推理时解决方案“Verbalized Sampling”，通过从言语化响应分布中采样来拓宽模拟器行为，减少模式崩溃；3. 提出训练时解决方案“Co-Training”，联合优化策略以对抗可训练的模拟器群体，防止对单一模拟器模式的过拟合。

Result: 在Persuasion for Good、$τ^2$-bench和CooperBench三个基准测试上验证：Verbalized Sampling使保留集成功率提升高达9%，Co-Training进一步提升至14%；人类研究显示对真实用户也有类似增益；两种方案均保持了策略多样性。发布了开源框架SCOPE用于群体协同训练多智能体强化学习。

Conclusion: 训练环境的多样性（而不仅仅是策略的多样性）对于多轮强化学习向现实世界部署的泛化能力至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [154] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合无监督聚类与监督分类的混合NLP框架，利用厄瓜多尔公共采购系统（SOCE）中的参与者评论元数据，以检测潜在的采购流程违规。通过领域适配的Word2Vec嵌入、高斯混合模型聚类及随机森林分类器，该方法在严重类别不平衡下实现了高精度和召回率，证明了轻量级NLP架构在提升公共采购透明度方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及巨额资金分配，需持续监管。然而，利益相关者评论和政府公开数据等潜在能揭示程序违规的信息往往未被充分利用。旨在填补这一空白，挖掘非结构化文本中的异常模式。

Method: 构建了集成自然语言处理（NLP）、无监督聚类和监督分类的混合建模框架。具体步骤包括：使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入；采用高斯混合模型（GMM）进行无监督聚类；最后应用监督分类器识别具有指控或举报性质的评论。实验重点评估了领域训练过的Word2Vec结合GMM和随机森林组合的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，结合领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入、基于GMM的聚类以及随机森林分类器的方法，即使在严重的类别不平衡情况下，也能实现高精度的预测和高召回率。

Conclusion: 轻量级且经过领域适配的NLP架构能够有效支持风险识别，增强公共采购系统的透明度，且无需依赖大规模计算基础设施。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [155] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出AI教育工具的基础设施（如训练语料、分词方案等）对孟加拉语等少数语言存在系统性劣势，而非单纯的技术局限。通过识别网络内容缺失、训练令牌赤字、分词惩罚及连接排斥四大结构性障碍，论证了数据集稀缺是资源分配不均的结果，并倡导将离线优先设计作为促进公平的基建策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示尽管AI工具被视为解决资源匮乏社区访问差距的方案，但其底层基础设施可能在模型训练前就对代表少数语言的群体造成系统性歧视。重点分析孟加拉语在低连接环境下的AI辅助教育所面临的结构性壁垒。

Method: 以孟加拉语为例，深入剖析其在AI辅助教育场景中的困境，具体识别并分析了四个相互关联的失败因素：全球网页内容占比极低（<0.5%）、主要多语言语料库中英语与孟加拉语的令牌数量巨大差异（67:1）、孟加拉语字母音节文字导致的分词效率低下，以及城乡互联网渗透率的显著差距。

Result: 研究发现这些失败反映了长期的资源分配决策、机构优先级设定和设计默认值未能以少数语言为中心。数据稀缺应被理解为一种结构性障碍，而非孤立的技术限制；同时，离线优先设计应被视为一种面向公平的基建策略。

Conclusion: 呼吁语言学及AI研究界关注并致力于减少这些结构性不平等，重新定义数据稀缺的性质，并将离线优先设计纳入公平导向的基础设施建设框架中。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Reinforcement Learning based DBMS Buffer Pool Auto-Tuning for Optimal Memory Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11239)
*Yifan Wang,Patrick Royer,Raphaël Féraud,David Delande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: MicroTune 是一种基于在线强化学习的数据库缓冲调整系统，旨在通过最小化不必要的内存分配来节省资源，同时确保满足服务等级协议（SLA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库管理员（DBA）在管理 DBMS 实例时，为了平衡 SLA 性能与资源使用，往往导致 RAM 过度分配，造成内存浪费。

Method: 引入 MicroTune 系统，评估多种强化学习算法在不同基准负载下的表现；利用外部指标（延迟、吞吐量）和内部 DBMS 指标（状态变量、性能统计）的大量轨迹进行训练，以动态调整缓冲区大小。

Result: 实验表明，MicroTune 能根据工作负载波动动态适应缓冲区大小，相比基线方法实现了显著的内存节省，并减少了 SLA 违规次数。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了强化学习在 DBMS 环境中自适应资源管理方面的前景。

Abstract: Administering Database Management Systems (DBMS) instances requires Database Administrators (DBA) to balance performance in terms of Service Level Agreement (SLA) against resource usage, often prompting RAM over-allocation that wastes memory. We introduce MicroTune, an online RL-based buffer adjustment system that minimizes unnecessary memory allocation while ensuring SLA compliance. To identify the most effective RL core, we evaluate multiple algorithms under diverse benchmark workloads, training MicroTune on extensive traces of both external metrics (latency, throughput) and internal DBMS metrics (status variables and performance statistics). Experimental results demonstrate that MicroTune dynamically adapts buffer sizes to workload fluctuations, outperforming baselines by achieving significant memory savings with fewer SLA violations. These findings underscore the promise of reinforcement learning for adaptive resource management in DBMS environments.

</details>


### [157] [Reverse Migration of Cloud Applications to On-premises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11640)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Diel工具，通过模拟、复制和委托策略自动化将云应用反向迁移至本地部署，解决合规行业需求与云端开发之间的矛盾，实现云和本地版本同步及快速迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限于安全和合规要求，监管行业倾向于本地部署，但开发者需在云端进行敏捷开发。这种矛盾导致发布周期长、维护复杂，急需一种自动化工具来弥合云端开发与本地部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 设计并实现名为Diel（Tursio On-premises Migrator）的工具，采用模拟（simulate）、复制（replicate）和委托（delegate）三种策略组合，将云服务转换为对应的本地部署环境实例。

Result: 成功保持Tursio AI的云版本和本地版本同步，将稳定版本的发布周期缩短至每三周一次，验证了该工具在实践中的有效性。

Conclusion: Diel有效解决了云开发与本地部署的悖论，通过自动化反向迁移流程，显著简化了多环境维护复杂度并加速了软件交付周期。

Abstract: Cloud has become ubiquitous to modern applications due to its agility and scalability. However, regulated industries still prefer to deploy on-premises due to security and compliance reasons. This creates a paradox for vendors who need to develop in the cloud but deploy on-premises, leading to long release cycles and complex maintenance. In this paper, we present Diel, the Tursio On-premises Migrator, a tool that automates reverse migration of cloud applications to on-premises environments. Diel applies a combination of simulate, replicate, and delegate strategies to transform cloud services into on-premises counterparts.We describe the design and implementation of Diel, along with lessons learned from using it in practice. With Diel, we have been able to keep Tursio AI's cloud and on-premises versions in sync, releasing new stable versions every three weeks.

</details>


### [158] [Guided Table Retrieval for Structured Data Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11644)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“引导式表格检索”的四阶段流水线，用于在结构化数据库上回答自然语言问题。该方法结合确定性基础定位、图结构探索、大语言模型消歧和算法合并，以生成精确的连接树，在BIRD-DEV和BEAVER基准测试中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在结构化数据库上回答自然语言问题时，识别相关表格并确定如何连接它们的需求。这一任务需要同时具备模式知识（Schema knowledge）和对用户意图的语义理解能力。现有的端到端大语言模型方法存在脆弱性，因此需要一种更稳健的方法。

Method: 提出了一个四阶段的引导式表格检索流水线：
1. 基于哈希预测器的确定性基础定位（Deterministic grounding）。
2. 通过连接图的可达性进行结构探索（Structural exploration）。
3. 利用大语言模型对源和目标进行消歧（LLM-powered disambiguation）。
4. 算法合并为最小化且拓扑有序的连接树（Algorithmic merging）。
该方法将问题分解为具有不同职责的阶段：确定性、覆盖率、语义推理和连贯性。

Result: 在BIRD-DEV基准测试上达到94%的精度和92%的F1分数；在企业级BEAVER基准测试上达到70%的精度和53%的F1分数。结果显著优于现有基线，并能直接生成下游查询编译器可消费的精确连接树。

Conclusion: 通过分阶段处理并结合确定性与大语言模型的上下文判断优势，该流水线避免了端到端LLM方法的脆弱性，有效解决了复杂数据库查询中的表格选择和连接问题，实现了高精度的性能表现。

Abstract: Answering natural language questions over structured databases requires identifying the relevant tables and determining how to join them---a task that demands both schema knowledge and semantic understanding of the user's intent.We present guided table retrieval, a four-phase pipeline that combines deterministic grounding via hash-based predictors, structural exploration of join-graph reachability, LLM-powered disambiguation of sources and targets, and algorithmic merging into minimal, topologically ordered join trees. By decomposing the problem into phases with distinct responsibilities--- determinism, coverage, semantic reasoning, and coherence--- the pipeline avoids the brittleness of end-to-end LLM approaches while leveraging LLMs where their contextual judgment is most needed. We evaluate on BIRD-DEV and the enterprise-scale BEAVER benchmark, achieving 94% and 70% precision respectively, with 92% and 53% F1---substantially outperforming existing baselines on precision and F1 while producing exact join trees that can be directly consumed by downstream query compilers.

</details>


### [159] [Enabling Differentiated QoS Degradation for Replicated Databases under Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11836)
*Belkis Djeffal,Pierre Bourhis,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PLB的PostgreSQL JDBC中间件负载均衡器，通过“修复至目标”策略，在副本发生故障导致容量损失时，动态分配健康副本为Premium、Mixed和Freemium角色，以在预算受限情况下维持差异化服务质量（QoS），显著提升了高优先级服务的吞吐量和延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弹性机制假设故障后可立即替换副本恢复容量，但在固定预算或操作受限条件下，容量恢复存在延迟且资源承诺额外。因此，服务必须在容量降低期间继续运行，且需显式控制降级行为，以维持不同服务等级之间的差异化，而非统一处理故障影响。

Method: 研究具有服务类感知会话的复制数据库服务中的差异化QoS降级问题。提出并实现了一种“修复至目标”（repair-to-target）策略，部署于PLB中间件中。当发生停止故障导致部分容量丢失时，PLB将健康副本的角色重新分配为Premium、Mixed和Freemium三类，确保新会话分配仍反映其服务类别，同时保持副本池共享。评估了单一故障和级联故障两种场景下的性能。

Result: 在Premium侧故障下，PLB使Premium服务的goodput保留率提高了26-28个百分点；在最严重的级联故障阶段，Premium goodput提升超过2倍；与共享轮询方式相比，Premium p95延迟降低了18.2%。

Conclusion: 在容量受限的故障场景中，通过服务类感知的动态角色分配策略，可以在不增加额外资源的前提下，有效保障高优先级服务的质量，证明差异化降级控制是应对复制系统故障的有效手段。

Abstract: Elasticity is commonly presented as the default response to capacity loss after failures, since replacement replicas can compensate for failed nodes and restore pre-incident service levels. Replacement capacity entails both delay and additional resource commitment, as replicas must be provisioned and synchronized before they can serve traffic. Under fixed budgets or constrained operating conditions, capacity restoration cannot be treated as the immediate recovery path. Failure handling must instead define how the service continues while capacity remains reduced. When the service exposes differentiated service levels, capacity loss cannot be handled uniformly. Degradation becomes part of the service behavior, requiring explicit control over how reduced capacity affects each class without erasing the intended differentiation.  We study differentiated QoS degradation in replicated database services with service-class-aware sessions. We present a repair-to-target policy, implemented in PLB, a PostgreSQL JDBC middleware load balancer for service-class-aware routing. When fail-stop failures remove part of the available capacity, PLB updates the role assignment of healthy replicas into Premium, Mixed, and Freemium roles. This keeps the replica pool shared while ensuring that new session assignments continue to reflect the service class. We evaluate PLB under single and cascading replica failures across two deployment strategies: isolated perclass replica pools and shared, priority-agnostic routing. The results show that PLB improves median Premium goodput retention by 26-28 percentage points under a Premium-side fault, achieves more than 2x higher Premium goodput in the most severe cascading-failure phase, and reduces Premium p95 latency by 18.2% relative to shared round-robin.

</details>


### [160] [DexterSQL: Deep Schema Exploration and Rule-based Correction for Text-to-SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11889)
*Anik Pramanik,Murat Kantarcioglu,Vincent Oria,Shantanu Sharma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DexterSQL 是一种基于提示（非微调）的 Text-to-SQL 系统，通过深度 Schema 探索、数据库无关规则创建和多路径 SQL 生成三个组件，解决了现有方法在细粒度关系识别、失败模式捕捉及复杂条件处理上的不足，在 BIRD-Dev 数据集上显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于提示（即不改变大语言模型参数）的 Text-to-SQL 方法面临三大问题：(i) 依赖粗粒度的 Schema 信息，无法揭示区分歧义列所需的细粒度关系；(ii) 未能捕捉重复出现的 SQL 生成失败模式；(iii) 在处理复杂问题时容易出现条件遗漏、幻觉或错位。

Method: 提出 DexterSQL 系统，包含三个新颖组件：(i) 深度 Schema 探索器：识别歧义列，分析其单独和联合数据分布以揭示关系及各自角色；(ii) 数据库无关规则创建者：仅在训练数据库上挖掘生成 SQL 与黄金 SQL 的不匹配，转化为捕获 LLM 常见失败模式的通用纠正规则；(iii) 多路径 SQL 生成：引入基于依赖树的中间表示，利用问题句子结构指导分解为 SQL 骨架，进而生成最终 SQL。

Result: DexterSQL 在开源/闭源权重模型上均取得了优于最先进方法的准确率。具体而言，在 BIRD-Dev 数据集上，使用开源模型 GPT-OSS-120B 时准确率至少提升 2.7%，达到 67.6%；使用闭源模型 GPT-4o 和 GPT-5.2 时准确率分别达到 71.6% 和 72.2%，提升至少 0.9%。

Conclusion: DexterSQL 通过引入细粒度 Schema 分析、通用错误纠正规则和多路径生成策略，有效克服了非微调 Text-to-SQL 方法的局限性，显著提高了 SQL 生成的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Prompting-based (\textit{i}.\textit{e}., non-fine-tuning) Text-to-SQL methods, where underlying large language model parameters are not changed for the task, face three problems: (\textit{i})~relying on coarse-grained schema information that may not reveal the fine-grained relationships needed to distinguish ambiguous columns, (\textit{ii})~not capturing recurring SQL-generation failures, and (\textit{iii})~suffering from omission, hallucination, or misplacement of conditions in complex questions.
  This paper develops \textsc{DexterSQL}, a prompting/non-fine-tuning-based Text-to-SQL system that improves SQL generation with three novel components: (\textit{i})~\emph{deep schema explorator} that identifies ambiguous columns, analyzes their individual and joint data distributions to uncover their relationships and the distinct role of each, (\textit{ii})~\emph{database-agnostic rule creator} that mines mismatches between generated and gold SQL only on the training database and converts them into database-agnostic corrective rules that capture recurring LLM failure patterns; and (\textit{iii})~\emph{multi-path SQL generation} that introduces a dependency-tree-based intermediate representation that uses the question's sentence structure to guide its decomposition into an SQL skeleton for final SQL generation.
  \textsc{DexterSQL} achieves a higher accuracy compared to the state-of-the-art using both open-source/weight and closed-source/weight models. Particularly, \textsc{DexterSQL}'s shows a high improvement of at least 2.7\% using an open-weight model (GPT-OSS-120B) on BIRD-Dev, with total accuracy 67.6\%. \textsc{DexterSQL} also shows better improvement of at least 0.9\% using closed-weight models, with total accuracy 71.6\% and 72.2\% on BIRD-Dev with GPT-4o and GPT-5.2.

</details>


### [161] [An Extended Tutorial and Vocabulary for Relational Language Design in an Era of AI-Assisted Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12272)
*Wolfgang Gatterbauer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该教程探讨了关系查询语言的设计与比较，重点关注机器生成、人类阅读场景下的语言可读性与结构显式性。通过抽象关系演算（ARC）和关系图作为参考基准，从示例出发构建了一套用于评估关系语言设计的词汇框架，涵盖递归/路径查询、嵌套数据及PTIME外问题，旨在帮助参与者理解不同语言在表达意图和设计权衡上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着查询日益由机器生成并由人类阅读，以及DataFrame、管道、逻辑等多种编程范式的兴起，现有研究多关注表达力，而忽视了哪些关系结构被语言显式化，以及符号表示如何支持用户阅读和修改查询。本教程旨在超越单纯的表达力讨论，深入探究语言设计对用户体验和结构清晰度的影响。

Method: 1. 从具体示例查询出发，而非形式化定义，对比不同语言如何表达相同意图；2. 使用抽象关系演算（ARC）和关系图作为参考表示，以比较不同符号系统中的 recurring structure（重复结构）；3. 结合递归/路径查询、嵌套关系数据和PTIME外问题的扩展方向，分析各类语言特性；4. 基于实例开发一套关系语言设计词汇表，包括信息需求、查询映射、关系模式结构等概念。

Result: 1. 建立了一套用于比较现有及未来关系语言的框架；2. 提供了精确的词汇体系，用于阐述关系语言设计中的权衡（trade-offs）；3. 提供了一组具体示例，连接经典数据库语言与替代性提案，涵盖更广泛的问题领域（如图查询、非PTIME问题）。

Conclusion: 关系查询语言的设计不仅关乎表达能力，更需考虑结构显式性和人机交互的可读性。通过引入ARC和关系图作为统一参考，并构建标准化的设计词汇，可以为开发者和分析者提供系统化的工具，以评估和优化不同关系语言在表达意图和支持用户操作方面的性能。

Abstract: Relational query languages have been studied and used for more than 50 years, with SQL dominant in practice. Today, queries are increasingly generated by machines and read by humans. At the same time, the landscape also includes dataframe, pipeline, logical, functional, graph, and relational programming notations. These developments invite two related questions beyond expressive power: which relational structures do languages make explicit, and how well can notation support users in reading and revising queries?
  This 3-hour tutorial extends an earlier SIGMOD'26 tutorial in three directions: recursive and path queries (connecting relational and graph query languages), nested relational data, and relational languages for problems beyond PTIME. Rather than beginning from formal definitions, we start from example queries and compare how different languages express the same intent. To compare recurring structure across notations, we use Abstract Relational Calculus (ARC) and Relational Diagrams as reference representations.
  From these examples, we develop a vocabulary for relational language design, including information need, query mapping, relational pattern structure, relational pattern denotation, and semantic conventions. Participants will leave with a framework for comparing existing and future relational languages, a precise vocabulary for articulating design trade-offs, and a concrete set of examples connecting classical database languages with alternative proposals.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [TRACES: A Benchmark for Epistemic Reliability in Scientific Reasoning by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11415)
*Valentin Rodionov,Shamil Assylbekov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在科学工作流中缺乏区分可靠与不可靠文献的能力。通过引入包含42篇问题论文的探针语料库，发现几乎所有模型都无法识别伪科学前提，其“拒绝”行为主要源于对知名话题的安全过滤而非真正的认知能力，表明当前LLM不适合直接作为无验证器的科学代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估已知答案问题的事实准确性，但无法衡量LLM在无下游验证器场景下区分可靠与不可靠科学文献的能力。本研究旨在填补这一空白，针对模型在面对有缺陷前提时的失败模式进行评估。

Method: 构建了一个包含42篇被撤稿、欺诈和伪科学论文的探针语料库。设计了一种方法论，从目标论文中提取前言并搭配科学上合理的研究设计请求，形成单次交互探针。涵盖五种声明类型：伪造观察、伪物理机制、魔法前提、合法化桥梁和照搬实验。使用两个互补分数（IFR-a衡量是否直接拒绝，IFR-i衡量是否识别不可靠性但仍参与）以及参与度深度指数（EDI）进行评分。

Result: 在30个模型和10次重复运行中，平均IFR-a为0.93，IFR-i为0.809。但在所有非空响应中，95%的模型参与了基于不可行前提的任务。超过71%的探针导致模型失败，22/30的模型失败率超过90%。模型的拒绝行为集中在少数高知名度话题和特定探针上，且在结构匹配控制下消失。

Conclusion: LLM对不可靠科学文献的处理表现一致于基于话题的安全行为，而非稳健的认识论能力。这表明目前LLM缺乏必要的认知胜任力来独立处理科学文献，迫切需要为科学部署建立护栏基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models are being proposed as agents in scientific workflows, in domains where no downstream verifier exists. Such deployment assumes the model can distinguish reliable scientific literature from unreliable literature, a capability that has not yet been directly measured. Existing benchmarks evaluate factuality on questions with known answers; the failure mode we target here is different. We introduce a probe corpus of 42 retracted, fraudulent, and pseudoscientific papers, paired with a methodology for eliciting and scoring single-shot model engagement with each paper's framing. Each probe pairs a preamble extracted near-verbatim from the target paper with a scientifically plausible study-design request. The probes span five claim types: fabricated observation, pseudophysical mechanism, magical premise, legitimization bridge, and cargo-cult experiment. Two complementary scores measure whether a model rejects the flawed premise outright (IFR-a) and whether it recognizes the unreliability while still engaging (IFR-i). A depth score, the Engagement Depth Index (EDI), quantifies reproduction of paper- or field-specific withheld details. Across 30 models and 10 repeated runs, aggregate IFR-a is 0.93 $\pm$ 0.004 and aggregate IFR-i is 0.809 $\pm$ 0.009. Models engaged with untenable premises in 95% of all non-empty responses. Every evaluated model fails more than 71% of agentic probes, and 22 of 30 models fail more than 90% of the time. Rejections are concentrated on a small number of high-notoriety topics and specific probes, and disappear under matched-structure controls. These results are consistent with topic-keyed safety behavior rather than robust epistemic competence, and indicate an urgent need for guardrail infrastructure for scientific deployment of language models.

</details>


### [163] [From Overlooked to Explored: Recovering Item Relations via Mixture of Perspectives for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11846)
*Junyoung Kim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Jaehyung Lim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对序列推荐中自注意力机制的相似性偏差问题，提出PRISM模块。通过多视角（亲和与对比）校准注意力，捕捉异构关系，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于Transformer的序列推荐模型中，点积注意力因相似性偏差而过度偏好相似物品，导致忽视包含重要偏好信号的异构关系，从而限制推荐性能的问题。

Method: 提出PRISM（Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module），利用K个视角透镜从不同 viewpoint 校准注意力。结合“亲和视图”优化同质关系和“对比视图”揭示被抑制的异构关系，以全面捕获用户偏好。

Result: 在七个真实世界基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，PRISM一致优于最先进（SOTA）的基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过多视角关系洞察合成模块有效缓解了自注意力的相似性偏差，提升了序列推荐的准确性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Capturing user preference from a user's interaction sequence is the central challenge of Sequential Recommendation (SR). This preference intuitively emerges from inter-item relations: each item transition reflects a preference embedded in the relations between items, making the faithful capture of these relations essential for accurate recommendation. For this reason, self-attention is dominant in sequential recommendation for its ability to compute pairwise item interactions, yet our empirical analysis reveals that it consistently suffers from similarity bias across various types of transformer-based SR models: dot-product attention scores disproportionately favor similar items, systematically overlooking heterogeneous relations with meaningful preference signals and directly limiting recommendation performance. To address this, we propose PRISM (Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module), a module that re-examines item relations from multiple perspectives. PRISM employs K Perspective Lenses to calibrate attention from distinct viewpoints, combining an Affinity View that refines homogeneous relations and a Contrast View that exposes heterogeneous ones suppressed by similarity bias, enabling the model to capture the full spectrum of user preferences. Extensive experiments on seven real-world benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/327aem/PRISM/.

</details>


### [164] [Sci-Surf: Navigating Scientific Literature Discovery through Human Feedback and Intelligent Summarizatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11973)
*Fang Guo,Qi Zhu,Rongcan Pei,Shuqi He,Hui Chen,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Sci-Surf系统，通过LLM用户画像和博客式多模态摘要解决学术发现中的意图建模与深度理解难题，在线评估显示预测对齐度提升10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学出版物快速增长导致研究人员难以识别相关新研究并有效理解；现有平台依赖静态主题或嵌入相似度，仅支持摘要/短总结，缺乏对细微意图的建模及深度论文总结能力。

Method: 构建以意图为核心的知识发现系统Sci-Surf，集成反馈驱动的个人化推荐与多模态博客式论文消化技术；利用基于大语言模型（LLM）的用户画像细化用户意图表示，并生成综合全文文本与视觉信息的结构化摘要。

Result: 演示了端到端的学术发现流水线；在为期一个月的在线真实用户评估中，结合言语化用户画像使推荐质量显著提升，预测与现实用户偏好的对齐度平均提高了10.4%，同时改善了论文消化质量。

Conclusion: Sci-Surf通过整合意图中心化的推荐机制和多模态内容生成，有效提升了学术发现的精准度和用户对新研究的深入理解能力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific publications makes it increasingly difficult for researchers to identify relevant new studies and effectively comprehend them. Existing academic discovery platforms typically rely on static topic subscriptions or embedding-based similarity and provide only abstracts or short summaries, offering limited support for nuanced intent modeling and in-depth paper summarization. We present Sci-Surf, an intent-centric knowledge discovery system that integrates feedback-driven personalized recommendation with multi-modal blog-style paper digestion. Our approach refines user intent representations through LLM-based user profiling, while generating structured summaries that synthesize textual and visual information from full papers. The demo presents an end-to-end academic discovery pipeline and demonstrates measurable improvements in both recommendation quality and digestion quality through real-user evaluations. Specifically, the integration of verbalized profiles led to a 10.4% average improvement in predictive alignment with real-world user preferences throughout a month-long online evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [HCGRec: Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation with Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11980)
*Kangning Zhang,Haotian Fang,Xukun Luo,Hao Yin,Yang Gao,Peng Yan,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HCGRec框架，通过提示条件生成解决语义ID推荐中的优化瓶颈，显著减少零优势样本并提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID生成器在基于奖励的后训练中存在结构化优化瓶颈：早期语义token若进入错误分支，有限 rollout 组难以到达真实物品，导致组相对优化获得相同的零奖励，无法产生有用优势信号。

Method: 提出HCGRec（Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation）框架：1) 使用检查点rollout诊断困难实例；2) 仅在生成器无法到达正确物品时提供最小目标前缀提示；3) 模型在提示分支下生成无提示后缀；4) 引入提示感知信用分解，对提示token使用监督学习保持语义/结构对齐，对采样后缀使用GRPO进行优化。

Result: 在序列推荐基准测试中，HCGRec显著优于监督微调和 vanilla 基于奖励的后训练方法；将零优势训练样本比例从70%以上降低至20%以下。

Conclusion: HCGRec通过提示机制恢复困难实例的学习信号，有效解决了语义ID生成推荐中的零奖励优化问题，提升了训练效率和模型性能。

Abstract: Semantic-ID generative recommenders represent each item as a short sequence of discrete semantic tokens and predict the next item by autoregressively generating this token sequence. This paradigm enables a unified generation interface for item IDs, histories, and item text, but it also creates a structured optimization bottleneck during reward-based post-training: when an early semantic token enters the wrong branch of the item-token space, finite rollout groups rarely reach the ground-truth item, so group-relative optimization receives identical zero rewards and produces no useful advantage. We propose Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation (HCGRec), a semantic-ID generative recommendation framework that recovers learning signal for such hard training instances. HCGRec diagnoses each instance with checkpoint rollouts and supplies a minimal target-prefix hint only when the current generator cannot reach the correct item. The model then generates the unhinted suffix under the hinted semantic branch, turning zero-reward groups into informative comparisons over item-token completions. Hinting also changes token identity: hinted prefix tokens are oracle-provided item context, while unhinted suffix tokens are sampled generation actions. We therefore introduce hint-aware credit decomposition, using supervised learning to preserve item-semantic and prefix-structure alignment for hinted tokens and GRPO to optimize the sampled suffix. Experiments on sequential recommendation benchmarks show that HCGRec substantially improves over supervised fine-tuning and vanilla reward-based post-training, while reducing zero-advantage training samples from over 70% to below 20%. The code is accessible at https://github.com/WncFht/GRec.

</details>


### [166] [Token-Level Credit Assignment Optimization for Generative Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12049)
*Xinpeng Zhao,Yang Liu,Ran Chen,Xinyu Ma,Daiting Shi,Pengjie Ren,Zhumin Chen,Zhaochun Ren,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Token级相关性奖励的细粒度强化学习框架，解决生成式检索中序列级反馈导致的信用分配粗糙问题，显著提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索模型仅在全序列生成后评估效果，导致Token级生成与文档级相关性监督不匹配；现有的强化学习方法依赖序列级奖励，无法精准识别哪些Token决策对检索成功或失败负责。

Method: 提出细粒度强化学习框架，通过衡量每个Token决策对预期检索质量的影响来估算逐步（step-wise）奖励，实现更精确的信用分配，并将此策略整合到策略优化框架中。

Result: 在检索基准测试中，该方法一致优于基于序列级奖励的基线模型，证明了细粒度监督在对齐自回归DocID生成与检索目标方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的Token级相关性奖励能有效改善生成式检索中的信用分配问题，提升模型检索性能。

Abstract: Generative retrieval models perform document retrieval by autoregressively generating document identifiers (DocIDs). This process naturally forms a sequential decision problem, where each decoding step selects a DocID token and the complete token sequence determines the retrieved document. However, retrieval effectiveness is typically evaluated only after the full DocID is generated, creating a mismatch between token-level generation and document-level relevance supervision. As a result, existing reinforcement learning methods for generative retrieval mostly rely on sequence-level rewards, where the same document-level feedback is propagated to all decoding steps. Such coarse-grained feedback makes it difficult to identify which token decisions are responsible for successful or failed retrieval.
  In this work, we propose a fine-grained reinforcement learning framework for generative retrieval with token-level relevance rewards. Instead of assigning a single reward to the entire generated DocID, we estimate step-wise rewards by measuring how each token decision changes the expected retrieval quality of the corresponding generation trajectory. This enables more precise credit assignment and encourages the policy to favor token decisions that contribute more directly to document-level relevance. We further develop practical reward estimation strategies tailored to the DocID generation process and incorporate them into a policy optimization framework. Experiments on retrieval benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms sequence-level reward baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of fine-grained supervision for aligning autoregressive DocID generation with retrieval objectives.

</details>


### [167] [Making Collaborative Signals Count: Graph-Aware Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12184)
*Fenglin Yan,Bohao Wang,Jian Zhang,Yu Cui,Tongya Zheng,Ye Feng,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GALLM，一种图感知大语言模型框架，通过在文本和物品标记上构建协作图并注入注意力偏置来解决LLM在推荐系统中难以捕捉全局协作信号的问题，在四个基准测试中取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）虽被广泛用于推荐系统，但其以语言为中心的预训练难以捕捉用户-物品交互中隐含的协作信号。现有方法要么依赖外部推荐器生成的表示，要么仅建模序列内部依赖，限制了利用全局协作模式的能力。

Method: 提出GALLM框架，在文本标记和物品标记上构建协作图，建模三种关系：Text-Text（保留语义依赖）、Item-Text（对齐物品与文本描述）、Item-Item（基于全局物品共现模式）。将这些关系转化为轻量级可学习注意力偏置，融入LLM注意力机制，无需额外图编码器即可实现协作感知的标记交互。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准测试上，GALLM的表现优于所有对比基线，在HR@5指标上平均比最强基线提升9.76%。

Conclusion: GALLM通过整合全局协作模式到LLM注意力机制中，有效解决了LLM在推荐任务中协作信号捕捉不足的问题，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted as backbones for recommender systems. However, their language-centric pretraining makes it difficult to capture collaborative signals implicit in user-item interactions, which are crucial for personalized recommendation. Existing methods either inject collaborative representations produced by external recommenders or model only intra-sequence dependencies, limiting their ability to exploit global collaborative patterns. To address this limitation, we propose GALLM, a graph-aware LLM framework for sequential recommendation. GALLM constructs a collaborative graph over text tokens and item tokens, and models three types of relations: Text--Text relations for preserving semantic dependencies, Item--Text relations for aligning item tokens with their textual descriptions, and Item--Item relations derived from global item co-occurrence patterns. These relations are transformed into lightweight learnable attention biases and incorporated into the LLM attention mechanism, enabling collaborative-aware token interactions without introducing an additional graph encoder. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that GALLM achieves the best performance among the compared baselines, improving over the strongest baseline by 9.76\% on average in HR@5.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制理论的治理层“经验协调器（EO）”，用于解决两个具有结构对立目标的LLM智能体在多轮交互中因缺乏共同目标函数而导致的对话崩溃问题。在模拟金融服务环境中，EO通过上下文博弈、PID控制器和POMDP信念跟踪器机制，显著提高了高意向顾问联系率（78.1% vs 46.1%），证实了治理策略对轨迹终点的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个LLM智能体具有结构上对立的目标且在多轮交互中互动时，由于缺乏共享的目标函数，往往导致对话崩溃而非良性竞争：访客方妥协，站点方停止调整策略，最终双方均未达成既定目标。旨在探索是否可以用控制理论治理层替代缺失的目标函数。

Method: 在模拟的金融服务环境中，构建一个站点智能体引导访客联系顾问，而访客保持心理现实主义抵抗力的场景。引入“经验协调器（EO）”作为治理层，包含三个核心机制：1) 基于现实网络分析的上下文博弈（CB）选择内容臂；2) PID控制器通过动态模式约束确保行为一致性；3) POMDP信念跟踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。进行了60,000次模拟实验。

Result: EO使高意向顾问联系率提升了32个百分点（78.1%对比基准的46.1%）。上下文博弈（CB）变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差，证明治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。 persona分析显示两种状态：对于无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是系统功能性的关键；对于已接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的同理心默认设置已足够。

Conclusion: 控制理论治理层能有效解决对立目标LLM智能体的交互崩溃问题。然而，所有发现均基于LLM-to-LLM模拟，PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性进行校准，未来需在实时流量上验证EO的有效性。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [169] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Distribird，一个基于多智能体管道的Web应用，自动化从科学文献中提取和拟合贝叶斯校准的先验分布。该工具通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布，具备可追溯性、本地运行以保护隐私，并在无文献时提供合理的非信息先验。评估显示其生成的先验质量与单提示LLM基线相当，且在处理范围外请求时更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管贝叶斯校准需要参数先验分布，但研究人员因构建信息丰富的先验耗时且需跨学科专业知识，通常仍依赖均匀先验。旨在解决这一痛点，实现先验构建的自动化。

Method: 开发Distribird系统：给定参数名、物理描述及领域背景，部署多智能体管道搜索文献，提取并加权报告值，利用AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。若无可查文献，则回退至非信息先验。使用Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B等开源模型进行评估，对比单提示LLM基线。

Result: 在24个参数、10个科学领域的测试中，完整流水线生成的先验质量与单提示LLM基线持平。所有先验均可追溯至具体文献；内置有效性层能拒绝生成范围外请求的先验（而基线在11/30案例中生成不可靠先验）；所有LLM调用均在本地运行，保障数据隐私。

Conclusion: 对于具有物理可解释参数的模型，Distribird提供了一种透明、安全且高效的先验构建方案。相比微小的点估计精度提升，其可追溯性、安全性和对无效请求的鲁棒性在科学应用中更为关键。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [170] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个自主AI研究代理对Conway 99图问题的系统性攻击。通过完全可复现的方法，证明了不存在满足特定约束的循环图和阿贝尔群图，并通过结构简化将问题转化为12正则图的搜索，最终验证了69.43%的最佳约束满足率，为证明该图不存在提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Conway 99图问题，即确定是否存在参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图。这是一个长期存在的组合数学难题，旨在通过自动化AI手段探索图论中的存在性问题。

Method: 1. 使用自主AI研究代理进行系统性、可复现的攻击；2. 对循环图 $\mathbb{Z}/99$ 和其他阶数为99的阿贝尔群进行穷举证明，分析其满足约束的比例上限；3. 实施强制结构简化，利用 $\lambda=1$ 和 $\mu=2$ 的性质将存在性问题坍缩为84个顶点的12正则图，并使用CP-SAT编码验证；4. 建立并验证基于指定自同构轨道的存在性框架。

Result: 1. 证明了任何阶数为99的循环图或阿贝尔群图最多只能满足68.0%的约束（33/49个差类）；2. 成功将原问题简化为在84个顶点上寻找12正则图的问题，并通过恢复唯一的srg(9,4,1,2)验证了该方法的有效性；3. 建立了有效的自同构轨道存在性检查框架；4. 获得了69.43%的最佳已验证约束满足率，且发现十四种不同方法均未超过此界限。

Conclusion: 由于任何低于总约束数4950的可证明界限都足以构成非存在性证明，而当前最佳结果为69.43%（远低于100%），这构成了强正则图srg(99,14,1,2)不存在的有力证据，表明该图极可能不存在。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [171] [RecSys Factory: Bounding LLM Agent Autonomy to Decision Points in the Industrial Recommender Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11241)
*Dongyang Ao,Kaixiang Fang,Shijie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“自主性-确定性-效率”三元悖论，并发布RecSys Factory平台，通过解构运行时和能力边界，在腾讯三个推荐业务线中部署78天，实现1624次工具调度及78.6%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型（LLM）智能体应用于工业级推荐系统时，面临通用自主性、工业确定性和端到端效率之间的冲突（三元悖论），需解决如何在保证稳定合规的前提下提升自动化水平的问题。

Method: 设计RecSys Factory平台，遵循“决策点自主而非流水线自主”原则：1. 运行时解构：利用事件源（如Hook、Webhook）避免长驻守护进程，等待期零CPU消耗；2. 能力解构：通过技能生态和PitfallStore限制自主性于预定义的类型化决策面；3. 人机协作：保留人类在诊断与执行边界的介入，采用审计追踪原语。

Result: 在腾讯三个异构推荐业务线连续运行78天，记录1,624次CLI工具调度，聚合成功率为78.6%。在两个业务线观察到入职时间压缩（作为案例观察而非普遍结论）。

Conclusion: 通过平衡自主性与确定性，该框架有效解决了工业推荐场景中LLM应用的稳定性与效率问题，验证了在人机协同架构下大规模部署LLM智能体的可行性。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents into industrial recommender operations exposes a three-way tension we frame as the autonomy-determinism-efficiency trilemma: general autonomy (interpreting operator intent, generating glue code zero-shot), industrial determinism (schema-conforming feature extraction, non-crashing A/B, zero compliance-path hallucination), and end-to-end efficiency. Any two can be maximized against the third. We present RecSys Factory, an LLM-agent platform deployed for 78 days across three heterogeneous Tencent recommender business lines. The design principle is autonomy at decision points, not over pipelines, made concrete through three deconstructions that each discharge one vertex of the trilemma. Runtime is deconstructed into three host-emitted event sources (Claude Code Stop hooks, corporate-IM webhooks, workflow scheduler APIs): the platform carries no long-running daemon during the wait phase and consumes zero CPU during the 94% of wall-clock spent waiting on Spark or GPU jobs. Capability is deconstructed into a 29-file skill ecosystem (8,971 lines of SKILL.md) whose per-skill pitfall tables mechanically compile into a 400-entry PitfallStore, confining autonomy to bounded typed decision surfaces inside pre-committed pipelines. Deployment spans three business lines with disjoint label semantics, A/B layer topologies, and operator personas; an onboarding-time compression is observed on two of the three and is reported as a case-study observation, not a generalization claim, and not measured against a controlled pre-platform baseline. The human is retained at the diagnostic-versus-execution boundary via a human-in-the-loop card protocol, deployed as an audit-trail primitive (schema-validated, idempotent, replayable) and reported from an 8-day 16-run pilot. Across the 78-day window the platform recorded 1,624 CLI-tool dispatches at a 78.6% aggregate success rate.

</details>


### [172] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MoE模型中4-bit KV缓存量化引发的路由翻转问题，发现约31%的量化损伤由路由介导。尽管检测器能识别翻转发生，但无法区分其有益或有害性，确立了针对此类特征的“收益-检测壁垒”，表明仅靠路由器统计信息难以实现选择性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由具有不连续性，部署中常用的4-bit KV缓存量化（通过受保护的BF16门读取）会导致令牌跨越决策边界，从而引发专家激活翻转。现有研究缺乏对此现象的系统性因果分析和量化评估。

Method: 提出了一套因果分析装置，包括：1) 定价路由介导分数（RMF）以量化量化损伤；2) 令牌级归因分解机制；3) 预注册的探针在三种架构上进行验证。实验涵盖模拟量化和真实int4 KV内核，所有假设、阈值和评估均在测量前预注册。

Result: 1) RMF约为0.31，即约三分之一的量化损伤由路由介导导致。2) 可部署的路由器边距检测器能以AUC 0.772检测翻转发生，但在判断翻转对损失的影响方向上处于随机水平，未发现有效的预测指标。3) 存在跨模型的“签名翻转税”和“符号不可分性”。4) 真实int4内核结果与模拟曲线兼容，但统计功效不足，未能独立复现具体数值。

Conclusion: 研究确立了针对路由器统计特征的选择性修复的检测极限（empirical benefit-detection barrier）。路由归一化约定主要调节损伤幅度而非恢复路由能力。该工作提供了对MoE量化鲁棒性的因果理解，并指出当前基于路由器状态的特征不足以指导有效的错误纠正策略。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [173] [Can Frontier LLMs Match Natively Multimodal Embeddings? A Comparison on Hard-Negative Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11343)
*Archan Dutta,Vyanktesh Kanungo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次直接对比了原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与基于大语言模型的视觉排序能力（GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4.6）。结果显示两者性能相当，但预计算嵌入更适合低延迟应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Google发布首个原生多模态嵌入模型Gemini Embedding 2，以及前沿LLM展现出强大的视觉理解能力，需要评估这两种范式在多模态检索中的相对有效性，特别是LLM能否作为有效的零样本排序器。

Method: 在Flickr30k数据集上，对原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与基于LLM的视觉排序方法（GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6）进行直接的零样本性能对比实验。

Result: GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6的性能与Gemini Embedding 2相当；但在嵌入预计算后，多模态嵌入模型在低延迟应用场景中表现更优。

Conclusion: 原生多模态嵌入模型与前沿LLM在多模态检索任务中具有可比性，用户可根据具体需求（如延迟敏感度）选择合适的技术路线。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

</details>


### [174] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种用低参数模型替代大语言模型（LLM）智能体以低成本模拟大规模社会的方法，通过[交互阶数×记忆]分类法预测代理误差，并在EconAgent等七个仿真中验证了理论预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模拟大量LLM智能体社会的高昂计算成本问题，因为此类模拟通常关注宏观现象（如相行为、典型事实及随智能体数量N的变化），而非单个智能体的认知细节。
